i Introduction

Rating Definitions

All ratings are based on Rise by Resistance   measurement     method. Ratings based on Embedded       Temperature  Device

(ETD) measurement may be provided under       application to  Cummins Generator Technologies.

Thermal Insulation Class

Insulation class governs the maximum permissible temperature an alternator can operate without damaging the insulation

system. Cummins Generator Technologies use Class H & Class F.

Insulation                                   Maximum Permissible

       Class                                  Temperature

Class F                                       155 ºC

Class H                                       180 ºC

Temperature Rise

Temperature rise is the increase  in  temperature above ambient      temperature  40  ºC.

Temperature Rise                              Temperature ºC

Class B                                       80 ºC

Class F                                       105 ºC

Class H                                       125 ºC

Standby application allows windings to run hotter than the Class H temperature rise limit, therefore  for  an  ambient    of

40 ºC temperature rise   150 ºC

27 ºC temperature rise   163 ºC

Generator Set Ratings

The tables below summarises the definitions according to ISO8528-1 for the generator set and a combination of

ISO8528-3 and IEC60034-1 for the alternator.

Genset Rating            Emergency Standby    Limited Time Prime     Prime Rated Power                     Continuous

(ISO8528-1)                       Power                       (LTP)               (PRP)               Operating Power

                                  (ESP)                                                                        (COP)

Load type                        Variable     Constant                   Variable                              Constant

Annual operating                  200                         500        Unlimited                             Unlimited

hours

Average load                      70%                         100%                70%                          100%

Overload                          No                          No                  10%                          No

                                                                     (1  hour in every 12      Hrs)

Alternator Rating                Standby                   Standby       Continuous                        Continuous

(NEMA MG1-32)

Duty Cycle                        S10                         S10                 S1                           S1

(IEC 60034-1)

                         Standby 150/40       Standby 150/40             Class H 125/40                    Class H 125/40

Alternator Ratings       Standby 163/27       Standby 163/27             Class F 105/40                    Class F 105/40

                                                                         Class B 80/40                     Class B 80/40

i Introduction

Derates

Ambient Temperature

If the ambient (alternator air inlet temperature)  exceeds  40  ºC   then  the   alternator     output  rating  must  be  reduced using the

following tables.

STAMFORD S0 to S7 (P7)

Temperature        ºC     Multiplying  Factor

45                              0.97

50                              0.94

55                              0.91

60                              0.88

STAMFORD P80              & S9

Utilisation               Class H                            Class F                            Class B

Temperature        ºC     Multiplying Factor         Multiplying Factor               Multiplying Factor

45                              0.970                           0.965                           0.955

50                              0.940                           0.930                           0.910

55                              0.910                           0.895                           0.865

60                              0.880                           0.860                           0.820

For ambient temperatures above 60 ºC please consult applications engineering.

Altitude (Low Voltage ≤ 690V)

All ratings are based on altitude up to 1000 metres  above      sea  level.  If  the  altitude  exceeds  1000m  then      the  alternator

output ratings must be derated using the following   table.

Altitude above sea        Multiplying Factor

level in metres

1500                            0.97

2000                            0.94

2500                            0.91

3000                            0.88

3500                            0.85

4000                            0.82

Please consult applications engineering department for

1. High Voltages (>690V)

2. Altitudes above 4000m
